Livability ~51% (family, location, condition, outdoor) · Financial / resale ~49% (price vs local €/m², value judgment, energy, running costs, tenure, legal). Tuned for a live-in home for one adult + a 3-yo, sold in 5–10 years. Price vs local €/m², family, location, energy (incl. upgrade-potential bonus), tenure, running costs and outdoor are computed from the data; value (a condition-adjusted judgment), condition and legal are scored by hand.
Conf. = data-verification confidence: how many of 6 key fields (price, area, WOZ, energy label, ground/tenure, beds) are verified against an independent source. ✓N/6, green ≥5 · amber ≥3 · red <3; ⚠ = a source conflict. Hover for which fields are unverified.

+15% ask		 82 3 1962 A Eigen grond ? €225 9 19
NEW via Funda digest 6-Aug-2026 (id 44555324). VERIFIED directly on live Funda listing. 1185 GJ Amstelveen, Augustinuspark buurt (district Keizer Karelpark) -- in scope. Maisonnette over 2 woonlagen (3e/4e verdieping), 82 m2 wonen + 5 m2 buitenruimte + 7 m2 externe berging, bouwjaar 1962, FULLY RENOVATED 2026 (PVC-visgraatvloer, gestuukte wanden, nieuwe luxe keuken met Siemens apparatuur, nieuwe badkamer, zwarte stalen binnendeuren) -- move-in ready, best condition in the current batch. 4 kamers (3 slaapkamers), 2 balkons (oost+west), eigen berging, optie los te koop parkeerplaats voor EUR20,000 (not included in asking price). GROUND: eigen grond / volle eigendom -- no erfpacht, a real plus vs most Amsterdam/Buitenveldert comps. ENERGY: label A officieel bevestigd -- top tier, no upgrade needed. LEGAL FLAG -- VvE CHECKLIST ALL 'NEE': despite charging EUR225/mo bijdrage, Funda's VvE checklist shows Inschrijving KvK=Nee, Jaarlijkse vergadering=Nee, Periodieke bijdrage=Nee, Reservefonds=Nee, Onderhoudsplan=Nee, Opstalverzekering=Nee -- this pattern (fee charged but no documented governance) suggests an informally run or recently reorganized VvE; get the VvE's KvK inschrijving, financial statements, MJOP and opstalverzekering polis before offering. Niet-zelfbewoningsclausule van toepassing (must live in it yourself -- buy-to-let blocked) and asbestclausule van toepassing (1962 bouwjaar, plausible asbestos presence, liability shifted to buyer) -- both fairly standard for this vintage but add to overall legal risk alongside the VvE gap. PRICE: EUR495,000 = EUR6,037/m2, ~13% ABOVE Augustinuspark buurt gemiddelde EUR5,340/m2 -- a real premium, though partially justified by the comprehensive 2026 renovation; no official WOZ record found for this address (recently renovated unit may not yet be re-assessed) -- WOZ figure above is an ESTIMATE only, treat the buurt-average comparison as the primary signal and get a WOZ-waardeloket check before offering. LOCATION: Augustinuspark/Keizer Karelpark is further east than most tracked Amstelveen comps -- distances ESTIMATED reusing nearby Keizer Karelpark-Oost comp (Franciscus van Assisielaan 27): ~9 min Emmakade 33, ~19 min Zuidas -- notably worse Zuidas access than most of the database. Listing itself notes tram 25 to Station Zuid ~25 min and 10 min fietsen to Stadshart. ACTION: verify VvE documentation (KvK, financials, MJOP, insurance) before offering; get an actual WOZ-waardeloket read; confirm whether the EUR20k parking spot is worth adding to the deal.

MAJOR FLAG -- FIXER-UPPER: 'Kluswoning' (officieel Funda-kenmerk), voormalige sociale huurwoning, 'dient volledig te worden gemoderniseerd' -- reken op EUR50-70k renovatiebudget (keuken, badkamer, vloeren, mogelijk deels elektra). ASBESTOS FLAG: 'niet-zelfbewonings- en asbestclausule van toepassing' -- gezien bouwjaar 1971 en de geplande volledige modernisering is een asbestinventarisatie voorafgaand aan sloop/verbouw noodzakelijk, dit kan extra kosten en vertraging opleveren. Ground: Volle eigendom (Kenmerken) = eigen grond, confirmed. PRICE: EUR339,000 = EUR3,767/m2 -- 31.5% onder buurtgemiddelde EUR5,502/m2, maar dit weerspiegelt vrijwel zeker de kluswoning-status/renovatiebehoefte, niet een pure bargain; na aftrek van geschat renovatiebudget (~EUR55k) komt de all-in prijs op ~EUR394k = EUR4,378/m2, nog altijd een redelijke korting. WOZ niet direct gevonden; geschat ~EUR400,000 (gemeentelijke waarde reflecteert doorgaans de 'normale' staat, niet het kluswoning-kortingseffect) -- ESTIMATE, verify. Bike ~10 min Emmakade / ~16 min Zuidas (estimated, reusing verified Groenhof 72 comp, same Kringloopbuurt). ACTION: vraag VvE-jaarrekening/MJOP/reservefonds op; laat een asbestinventarisatie en bouwkundige keuring uitvoeren voor bod; begroot renovatie zorgvuldig.

NEW via Funda digest 4-Aug-2026 (id 44444063). VERIFIED directly on live Funda listing. 1082 EN Amsterdam, Buitenveldert-Zuid-Midden -- in scope (1082 postcode). Portiekwoning over 2 woonlagen, 97 m2 wonen + 36 m2 achtertuin (zuidwest) + 6-7 m2 balkon + inpandige berging, bouwjaar 1963, 5 kamers (4 slaapkamers) -- unusually large/family-sized for this database, no bedroom cap per current brief. LEGAL: erfpacht afgekocht tot 01-09-2051 (~25 jaar runway) -- materially LOWER erfpacht risk than most tracked Buitenveldert comps (which cluster around 2035-2039 revisions). Asbestinventarisatie uitgevoerd, saneringsplichtig asbest reeds verwijderd -- historical issue but already resolved, note for buyer due diligence only. RESTRICTIONS: zelfbewoningsclausule (2 jaar) en niet-bewoningsclausule worden opgenomen in de koopovereenkomst -- buy-to-let/flip is blocked for 2 years, fine for owner-occupier use but rules out immediate rental. PRICE FLAG -- LOW-ASK BIDDING RISK: EUR500,000 = EUR5,155/m2, ~21% BELOW buurt gemiddelde EUR6,504/m2 (Buitenveldert-Zuid-Midden) and roughly at par with a 2020 WOZ of EUR516,000 that would very plausibly have risen to well over EUR600k-650k by now given ~5 years of Amsterdam appreciation -- this combination (large family unit, priced well under both buurt average and an inflation-adjusted WOZ) strongly suggests a deliberately low asking price to attract many bidders ('bieden vanaf') rather than genuine underpricing; expect the property to sell well above vraagprijs. Budget accordingly and do not anchor on the EUR500k asking figure. ENERGY: label D, blokverwarming, no insulation/HR++ details given -- below-average, real upgrade potential (D->B/A plausible with double glazing + individual heating retrofit) but not yet reflected in price. CONDITION: dichte (closed) keuken, no renovation mentioned in the listing, 1963 bouwjaar with only a D label -- likely dated finish, budget for a kitchen/bathroom refresh. VvE: bijdrage EUR316.18/mo excl. EUR85.07/mo stookkosten voorschot (total ~EUR401/mo) -- notably higher than typical Buitenveldert comps (~EUR200-220/mo), checklist volledig groen (KvK, jaarvergadering, reservefonds, onderhoudsplan, opstalverzekering allemaal Ja); VvE also expects to connect to stadswarmte in the near future (extra one-off cost likely). LOCATION: distances estimated reusing verified Buitenveldert-Zuid comps (Holy 31 area) -- ~14 min Emmakade 33, ~7 min Zuidas (Station Zuid noted as ~10 min fietsen in listing text, consistent). ACTION: treat vraagprijs as a bidding floor, not a target; get a same-day WOZ-waardeloket check and a structural/asbestos due-diligence confirmation before offering.

SEVERE LEGAL RISK -- this is a former Omnia Wonen social-housing rental (leeggekomen huurwoning) with a statutory priority scheme: qualifying social-housing tenants get first right to buy until 25-08-2026, 12:00, so Max most likely cannot even secure this property before that date, and even after, competing priority claims could still apply. On top of that: a self-occupancy clause (buyer or 1st-degree family must live there as main residence for >=3 years), an anti-speculation clause (5 years), a prohibition on resale (>=3 years), and asbestos / non-owner-occupancy / age-of-building clauses (relevant given the 1963 build) all apply, and the seller retains discretion over buyer selection (recht van gunning) rather than a plain highest-bid sale. legal=2 -- this trips the project's legal<4 veto gate (v5 model), which caps the overall weighted score at 5.4/red regardless of how well it scores elsewhere; treat as effectively disqualified unless Max specifically wants to pursue a social-housing-priority route. On the plus side: eigen grond confirmed directly in the listing ('Gelegen op eigen grond') -- no erfpacht -- and energy label B is the best of this batch, with HR++ glazing replaced in 2025. Interior otherwise needs a full personal fit-out ('naar eigen smaak in te richten') -- dated, no other recent updates. Distances: ~10 min Emmakade (matches the two William Boothlaan comps already in this DB, same street), ~19 min Zuidas -- Emmakade proximity is excellent, easily the best of this batch. value/condition hand-scores intentionally left blank (renormalise), consistent with the 30-entry backlog from the 2026-07-31 QA review, though given the veto gate the overall ranking is capped regardless.

On top of that: a self-occupancy clause (buyer or 1st-degree family must live there as main residence for >=3 years), an anti-speculation clause (5 years), a prohibition on resale (>=3 years), and asbestos / non-owner-occupancy / age-of-building clauses (relevant given the 1963 build) all apply, and the seller retains discretion over buyer selection (recht van gunning) rather than a plain highest-bid sale. legal=2 -- this trips the project's legal<4 veto gate (v5 model), which caps the overall weighted score at 5.4/red regardless of how well it scores elsewhere; treat as effectively disqualified unless Max specifically wants to pursue a social-housing-priority route. On the plus side: eigen grond confirmed directly in the listing ('Gelegen op eigen grond') -- no erfpacht -- and energy label B is the best of this batch, with HR++ glazing replaced in 2025. Interior otherwise needs a full personal fit-out ('naar eigen smaak in te richten') -- dated, no other recent updates. Distances: ~10 min Emmakade (matches the two William Boothlaan comps already in this DB, same street), ~19 min Zuidas -- Emmakade proximity is excellent, easily the best of this batch. value/condition hand-scores intentionally left blank (renormalise), consistent with the 30-entry backlog from the 2026-07-31 QA review, though given the veto gate the overall ranking is capped regardless.

Methodology (model v5): Weighted score out of 10, tuned for a live-in home for one adult + a 3-yo (shared custody), sold in 5–10 years. Two clusters — livability ~51%: Family fit & space (17%), Location & commute (15%), Condition (13%), Outdoor space (6%); financial / resale ~49%: Price vs local €/m² (9%), Value judgment (10%), Energy label (10%), Running costs (8%), Tenure / erfpacht (7%), Legal / title risk (5%). v4 change: €/m² now outweighs the raw WOZ premium — Price vs local €/m² is the asking-vs-WOZ premium centred on the property's city median, so WOZ serves only as the location/size-aware normaliser, not a stale absolute anchor. Running costs are the full owner monthly = VvE + heating advance + WOZ-driven owner taxes (OZB ≈0.063% Amstelveen / ≈0.058% Amsterdam + eigenwoningforfait 0.35% × ~37% box-1), so a higher WOZ raises carrying cost. Price vs €/m², family, location, energy, tenure, running costs and outdoor are computed from the underlying data; value (a condition-adjusted judgment overlay: over-priced-for-condition, bidding-war temper), condition and legal are scored by hand against the rubric. Blank criteria renormalise over the ones that are scored. WOZ values are the official 2025 Kadaster LV-WOZ assessments; OZB/forfait are 2025 rates (tunable) — re-check at offer time. v5 change: veto gates — legal <4, Schiphol LIB zone, price >€550k (budget) or 55+ cap the total at 5.4 (⛔, raw score in tooltip): dealbreakers no longer average away. Running costs add the erfpacht canon; a VvE fee <€100/mo without a verified reserve caps the costs score (underfunded-reserve risk). Location blend Emmakade/Zuidas = 70/30. The relvalue city-median baseline is pinned per quarter. All data as of 2026-08-24.
